# Unified TCC test manifest
# Format: type:path:flags:expected
#
#   run      compile + assemble + run; expected = exit code
#   error    compile only, expect failure; expected = stderr substring
#   warn     compile, check stderr contains substring; expected = substring
#   nowarn   compile with flags, check stderr is empty; expected = (empty)
#   dwarf    compile -S, check asm contains patterns; expected = space-separated grep patterns
#   todo     like run but non-gating (SKIP on failure)
# Paths are relative to tests/. Flags are passed directly to the compiler.
# For run/todo tests the global -target is used unless overridden in flags.
# For dwarf tests use -target=arm64 or -target=x64 in flags to fix the target.
